BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority , Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within 30
days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, Executive Engineer,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division 14 Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
Wadigodri. North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority , Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within 30
days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, Executive Engineer,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division 14 Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
Wadigodri. North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority , Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within 30
days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, Executive Engineer,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division 14 Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
Wadigodri. North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority , Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within 30
days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, Executive Engineer,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division 14 Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
Wadigodri. North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority , Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within 30
days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, Executive Engineer,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division 14 Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
Wadigodri. North, Paithan.

NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority , Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within 30
days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, Executive Engineer,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division 14 Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
Wadigodri. North, Paithan.

days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority , Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within 30
days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, Executive Engineer,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division 14 Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
Wadigodri. North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority , Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within 30
days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, Executive Engineer,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division 14 Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
Wadigodri. North, Paithan.

DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority , Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, Executive Engineer,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division 14 Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
Wadigodri. North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority , Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within 30
days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, Executive Engineer,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division 14 Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
Wadigodri. North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority , Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, Executive Engineer,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division 14 Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
Wadigodri. North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIVISION 14
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority , Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, Executive Engineer,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division 14 Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
Wadigodri. North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 1, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Divisional No. 1 Executive Engineer,
Nathnagar (N) Paithan. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 1, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Divisional No. 1 Executive Engineer,
Nathnagar (N) Paithan. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Divisional No. 1 Executive Engineer,
Nathnagar (N) Paithan. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 1, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Divisional No. 1 Executive Engineer,
Nathnagar (N) Paithan. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 1, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Divisional No. 1 Executive Engineer,
Nathnagar (N) Paithan. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 1, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Divisional No. 1 Executive Engineer,
Nathnagar (N) Paithan. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 1, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Divisional No. 1 Executive Engineer,
Nathnagar (N) Paithan. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 1, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Divisional No. 1 Executive Engineer,
Nathnagar (N) Paithan. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 15, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division No. 15, Executive Engineer,
Tirthpuri. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 15, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division No. 15, Executive Engineer,
Tirthpuri. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 15, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division No. 15, Executive Engineer,
Tirthpuri. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 15, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division No. 15, Executive Engineer,
Tirthpuri. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 15, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division No. 15, Executive Engineer,
Tirthpuri. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER, JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION,
NORTH, PAITHAN
DIVISION : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION DIVISION , NORTH, PAITHAN.
Sub Division : JAYAKWADI IRRIGATION SUB DIV NO. 15, NORTH, PAITHAN
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User
Associations (WUA’s) on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005, I P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, Jayakwadi
Irrigation Division,North, Paithan hereby delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s
Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of gut numbers and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned
Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, P.B. Jadhav, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system
of supply of water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the
occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days
from the date of publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending
Engineer, Command Area Development Authority, Chhatrapati Sambhajinagar within
30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
Sub Divisional Engineer, P. B. Jadhav,
Jayakwadi Irrigation Sub Division No. 15, Executive Engineer,
Tirthpuri. Jayakwadi Irrigation Division,
North, Paithan.
